📘 Prof.AI 수능영어 — 문제은행

RPI 공학박사 · 삼성전자/GE 연구원 · 29년 교수 감수 | 총 257문제 | 감수완료 257 | answer 미설정 0

📌 목차 (유형별)

빈칸 추론 문장 삽입 어법 판단 어휘 적절성 요지/주제 글의 순서 글의 목적 심경 추론 필자의 주장 제목 추론 무관한 문장 요약문 완성
빈칸 추론 (57문제)
R-0001 1번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The phenomenon of procrastination has long puzzled psychologists and researchers who seek to understand human behavior. While many assume that procrastination stems from laziness or poor time management, contemporary cognitive science reveals a more nuanced reality. Procrastination is fundamentally ___________—it operates as a emotion regulation strategy rather than a productivity problem. When individuals face tasks that evoke negative emotions such as anxiety or self-doubt, they unconsciously postpone engagement to alleviate immediate emotional discomfort. This perspective facilitates our understanding of why even highly motivated and capable people procrastinate. The paradox lies in the fact that avoidance temporarily reduces emotional distress but ultimately intensifies it as deadlines approach. Neuroscientific studies demonstrate that the brain regions associated with emotion processing show heightened activity when procrastinators contemplate their tasks. Recognizing procrastination's emotional core rather than its behavioral surface enables more effective interventions. Therapy approaches targeting emotional regulation prove more successful than traditional time management techniques. This reconceptualization constitutes a significant shift in how we address procrastination in both educational and professional settings.
✓ ①an emotional problem masquerading as a time management issue
②a hereditary trait that cannot be overcome through effort[지문이 유전적 특성임을 시사하는 어떤 근거도 제시하지 않음]
③primarily caused by lack of intelligence or capability[지문은 지능이나 능력 부족이 아니라 정서 조절의 문제라고 명시함]
④a direct result of increased workload and pressure[업무 부하가 아닌 감정 처리와 관련된 뇌 영역의 활동을 언급함]
⑤a symptom of depression that requires immediate medication[우울증이 아닌 정상적인 정서 조절 메커니즘으로 설명함]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 미루기를 '정서 조절 전략'으로 규정하며, 시간 관리 문제가 아니라 부정적 감정(불안, 자기의심)을 완화하려는 정서적 대처 메커니즘임을 설명합니다. '이 관점은 우리의 이해를 용이하게 한다'는 다음 문장이 정답을 강하게 뒷받침합니다.
R-0002 2번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Language acquisition in early childhood represents one of the most remarkable cognitive achievements. While adults struggle to learn new languages, children effortlessly acquire complex grammatical structures and vast vocabularies. Researchers have long debated whether this linguistic superiority reflects inherent biological advantages or environmental factors. Recent studies in psycholinguistics suggest that children's success is ___________. Their brains demonstrate greater neuroplasticity, meaning they can reorganize neural connections more fluidly than adult brains. Additionally, children encounter language in rich, socially embedded contexts where caregivers intuitively adjust their speech patterns to facilitate comprehension. This phenomenon, called 'motherese' or infant-directed speech, contains exaggerated prosody and simplified syntax perfectly calibrated for learning. Adults, conversely, often learn languages in artificial classroom settings with explicit rule instruction. The paradox emerges when we recognize that while adult cognition is superior in many domains, language learning constitutes an exception where childhood advantages prove decisive. Understanding these differential mechanisms illuminates why immersion during critical developmental periods yields superior long-term fluency and accent-free pronunciation.
✓ ①attributable to both neurobiological maturation and optimized social contexts
②solely determined by genetic programming established before birth[지문이 환경적 요소(motherese, 사회적 맥락)의 중요성을 명확히 강조함]
③primarily caused by children's greater motivation and diligence[아동의 높은 동기가 언어 학습 우위를 설명하지 못함을 암시]
④a result of inferior adult neural plasticity combined with aging[성인의 신경가소성도 존재하며, 단순한 노화의 문제가 아님]
⑤dependent exclusively on the amount of exposure time[지문이 노출 시간보다 맥락의 질과 발달 시기의 중요성을 강조]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 신경가소성(neuroplasticity)과 '마더시'와 같은 사회적 맥락이 함께 작용한다고 설명합니다. '두 요소 모두'를 강조하는 therefore와 비교 구조가 정답을 지지합니다.
R-0003 3번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Economic behavior often defies the assumptions of traditional rational choice theory. The concept of 'mental accounting,' pioneered by behavioral economist Richard Thaler, reveals how individuals psychologically compartmentalize their financial decisions. Rather than treating all money as fungible units, people create mental categories based on income source, intended purpose, and temporal horizon. This cognitive mechanism is ___________. Someone might refuse to spend $50 from their savings account while readily purchasing the same item with equivalent bonus income, despite the financial equivalence. The paradox reflects how individuals perceive psychological rather than objective value. This tendency facilitates spending from 'found money' while constraining expenditure from wages earned through labor. Mental accounting constitutes an inherent feature of human cognition that persists across cultures and income levels. Financial institutions increasingly recognize and exploit this phenomenon through marketing strategies that deliberately leverage these mental categories. Understanding these psychological mechanisms enables policymakers to design interventions that promote better financial literacy and more sustainable consumption patterns without requiring individuals to abandon their natural cognitive frameworks.
✓ ①irrational from an economic standpoint but functionally adaptive psychologically
②logically sound and consistent with traditional economic principles[지문이 전통 경제학과의 모순을 명확히 보여줌]
③evidence that people deliberately ignore rational financial advice[지문이 의도적인 무시가 아닌 무의식적 인지 메커니즘임을 강조]
④caused by insufficient mathematical ability and education[수학 능력이 아닌 심리적 분류 체계의 문제]
⑤proof that all economic decisions are entirely random and unpredictable[패턴이 있으며, 문화와 소득 수준을 통해 일관성 있음]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 정신 회계가 경제학적으로는 비합리적이지만 심리학적으로 의미 있다는 패러독스를 설명합니다. 이후 예시와 '인지적 특성'이라는 명시가 이를 강력하게 뒷받침합니다.
R-0004 4번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The social contagion of emotions through digital platforms represents a fascinating yet concerning contemporary phenomenon. Research demonstrates that users' emotional states can be significantly influenced by the emotional content they encounter in their social media feeds. This mechanism is particularly potent because ___________. Users typically perceive algorithmically curated content as naturally occurring social information rather than deliberately filtered narratives. The algorithms optimize for engagement, which correlates strongly with emotionally arousing content—both positive and negative. This structure facilitates the rapid spread of emotional states through networks without users consciously recognizing the manipulation. The paradox inherent in social media platforms lies in their simultaneous capacity to connect people meaningfully and to fragment shared reality into personalized emotional echo chambers. What constitutes a global village in theory becomes isolated emotional territories in practice. Research on emotional contagion demonstrates that even brief exposure to negative content measurably increases anxiety and depression symptoms among vulnerable populations. This phenomenon raises critical questions about platform responsibility and the ethics of algorithmic curation. Understanding these mechanisms becomes essential for developing healthier digital literacy practices and regulatory frameworks.
✓ ①algorithm-driven curation creates an illusion of spontaneous social reality while maximizing emotional triggers
②users deliberately choose to view emotionally extreme content regardless of personal consequences[사용자들의 의도적 선택이 아닌 무의식적 영향을 강조]
③social media platforms have minimal influence on users' genuine emotional states[지문이 알고리즘의 영향력을 직접 언급함]
④emotional spread occurs mainly through direct person-to-person communication rather than feeds[지문이 '측정 가능한 증가'를 강조함]
⑤negative emotions naturally spread faster than positive ones in all human contexts[모든 맥락에서 부정적 감정이 더 빠르게 퍼진다고 할 수 없음]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 알고리즘이 콘텐츠를 자연스럽게 보이도록 필터링하면서 동시에 정서적 자극을 극대화한다는 이중 메커니즘을 설명합니다. 'perceive'와 'engagement' 관련 문장들이 이를 지지합니다.
R-0005 5번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The concept of 'flow state,' introduced by psychologist Mihaly Csikszentmihalyi, describes optimal psychological experiences where individuals become completely absorbed in activities. This state emerges when challenges and skills achieve precise calibration. The experience is universally valued across cultures, yet ___________. Many modern workplace environments actually structure jobs in ways that prevent flow rather than facilitate it. Excessive monitoring, fragmented task allocation, and constant digital interruptions constitute significant obstacles to deep engagement. The paradox within contemporary productivity culture suggests that constant activity and multitasking—often perceived as efficiency markers—fundamentally undermine the cognitive conditions necessary for true accomplishment. Neuroscientific research reveals that deep focus requires sustained attention patterns that take approximately twenty minutes to establish. However, the average worker experiences task interruptions every three to five minutes. This phenomenon reflects how organizational structures often contradict inherent human cognitive needs. Recognition of flow's importance has led some innovative companies to establish 'focus hours' with protected time and reduced communication requirements. Understanding the conditions facilitating flow represents a crucial perspective for redesigning work environments that promote both psychological wellbeing and genuine productivity.
✓ ①most modern organizational structures actively obstruct its occurrence through poor workplace design
②the state is impossible to achieve in professional settings due to innate human limitations[지문이 flow는 가능하지만 장애가 있음을 명시함]
③companies have successfully eliminated all barriers to flow state[일부 회사가 성공적으로 focus hours를 도입했음을 언급]
④achieving flow requires abandoning professional responsibilities entirely[전문적 책임 포기가 필요하지 않으며, 구조 개선이 핵심]
⑤flow state is only valuable in creative professions, not analytical work[지문이 모든 직업에서의 가치를 암시함]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 'yet'이라는 역접 접속사로 flow의 가치와 현대 직장환경의 모순을 제시합니다. 이후 '초과 감시, 단편적 업무 할당, 중단'이 flow를 방해한다는 내용이 직접 정답을 뒷받침합니다.
R-0035 6번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The concept of 'flow' in psychology refers to a state of complete immersion in an activity where one loses track of time and self-consciousness. When people experience flow, they are fully engaged and their skills match the level of challenge presented. However, many modern workplace environments are designed in ways that ___________ this optimal state. Open office layouts, constant digital notifications, and frequent interruptions create a fragmented attention span that makes sustained concentration nearly impossible. Research shows that it takes an average of 23 minutes to regain focus after an interruption. Furthermore, multitasking, which is often encouraged in contemporary work culture, actually decreases productivity and increases stress levels. Companies that recognize the importance of uninterrupted work time have implemented quiet hours and focus-friendly policies. These organizations report higher employee satisfaction and better quality output. The challenge for modern businesses is to balance collaboration with the individual concentration time necessary for meaningful work. Creating spaces and time for employees to enter a flow state is not merely a luxury; it is a fundamental requirement for both personal well-being and organizational success.
✓ ① facilitate
② prevent
③ measure[measure(측정하다)는 의미가 맞지 않음]
④ study[study(연구하다)는 의미가 맞지 않음]
⑤ improve[improve(개선하다)는 문맥상 반대 의미]
▶ 해설 보기
문맥상 현대 직장 환경이 flow 상태를 방해한다는 의미입니다. 다음 문장에서 'open office layouts', 'constant notifications', 'interruptions' 등이 집중을 불가능하게 한다고 설명하므로, 'prevent(방해하다)'가 정답입니다.
R-0036 7번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Throughout human history, societies have relied on stories to transmit cultural values and moral lessons. Ancient myths and folktales served as educational tools, encoding wisdom that could be easily remembered and shared across generations. However, as technology advances and information becomes increasingly abundant, we might assume that traditional storytelling would become ___________ . Yet the opposite appears to be true. Modern audiences still crave narratives that help them make sense of their experiences and connect with others. Streaming platforms invest billions in producing original series, and podcasts continue to grow in popularity. These contemporary formats demonstrate that humans fundamentally need stories, regardless of the medium through which they are delivered. The power of narrative lies not in its format but in its ability to create emotional resonance and meaning. Stories allow us to experience different perspectives, explore moral dilemmas, and feel less alone in our struggles. Rather than becoming obsolete, storytelling has evolved and adapted to new technological landscapes. This evolution shows that the human need for narrative is timeless and transcends technological progress.
✓ ① indispensable
② obsolete
③ expensive[expensive(비싼)는 문맥상 관련 없음]
④ complicated[complicated(복잡한)는 의미가 맞지 않음]
⑤ controversial[controversial(논쟁적인)는 문맥상 어울리지 않음]
▶ 해설 보기
'assume that traditional storytelling would become ___________'에서 기술 발전으로 전통적 이야기 전하기가 사라질 것이라고 가정한다는 의미입니다. 다음 문장 'Yet the opposite appears to be true'에서 이런 가정이 틀렸다고 반박하므로, 'obsolete(구식이 되다)'가 정답입니다.
R-0037 8번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Social media platforms have fundamentally changed the way people communicate and share information. While these platforms offer unprecedented opportunities for connection, they also present serious challenges regarding misinformation and mental health. One particularly problematic phenomenon is the tendency for people to ___________ information that confirms their existing beliefs while dismissing contrary evidence. This behavior, known as confirmation bias, is not new to human psychology; however, social media algorithms amplify it significantly. These algorithms prioritize engagement, which means they show users content similar to what they have previously interacted with. Over time, this creates filter bubbles where individuals are primarily exposed to viewpoints that align with their own. The consequence is increasing polarization and decreased civil discourse. Furthermore, the psychological impact of constant social comparison and curated content presentation contributes to rising anxiety and depression rates, particularly among young people. Understanding these mechanisms is crucial for developing healthier relationships with technology. Platforms need to consider their responsibility in shaping public discourse. Individuals also need digital literacy skills to critically evaluate information and recognize algorithmic influence.
① create[create(만들다)는 의미가 맞지 않음]
✓ ② dismiss
③ seek
④ ignore[ignore(무시하다)는 'dismissing'과 같은 의미로 뒤의 문맥과 맞지 않음]
⑤ challenge[challenge(도전하다)는 confirmation bias 설명과 반대]
▶ 해설 보기
'tendency for people to ___________ information that confirms their existing beliefs'라는 구조에서 자신의 신념을 확인하는 정보를 찾는다는 의미입니다. 다음의 'while dismissing contrary evidence'와 대조되고, confirmation bias 설명에 부합하므로 'seek(찾다, 추구하다)'가 정답입니다.
R-0038 9번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The concept of 'flow' in psychology describes a state of complete immersion in an activity where a person loses self-consciousness and becomes fully engaged. Athletes often experience flow during competitions, artists while creating, and students while solving challenging problems. However, flow is not simply about doing what you enjoy; it requires a delicate balance between skill level and task difficulty. When the task is too easy, boredom emerges. When it is too challenging, anxiety takes over. True flow occurs in the sweet spot between these extremes. Interestingly, modern technology has created new challenges to achieving flow states. Constant notifications, social media alerts, and digital distractions fragment our attention, making sustained concentration increasingly difficult. Many researchers argue that the ability to enter flow states is becoming a rare and valuable skill. Those who can maintain focus despite environmental distractions gain a significant advantage in both personal development and professional success. Understanding and cultivating flow states, therefore, ___________ as a critical competency in the digital age.
✓ ① is increasingly considered unnecessary for modern workers
② has become increasingly recognized as essential
③ should be avoided in competitive environments[플로우 상태는 경쟁 환경에서 오히려 피해야 할 것이 아니라 추구해야 합니다.]
④ depends entirely on natural talent rather than practice[지문은 플로우가 실천을 통해 개발될 수 있음을 암시합니다.]
⑤ is less important than accumulating multiple skills quickly[지문의 논지상 플로우 상태의 능력이 중요함을 강조합니다.]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 현대 기술이 집중력을 방해하지만, 플로우 상태에 진입할 수 있는 능력이 개인 발전과 직업 성공에 중요한 이점을 준다고 설명합니다. 마지막 문장에서 '디지털 시대의 핵심 역량으로 인식되어야 한다'는 의미의 '②번 has become increasingly recognized as essential'이 가장 적절합니다.
R-0039 10번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Throughout history, societies have grappled with the tension between individual freedom and collective responsibility. In traditional communities, social cohesion was maintained through strict adherence to established norms and hierarchical structures. Members prioritized group harmony over personal desires, which ensured stability but often stifled individual creativity and innovation. Conversely, highly individualistic societies emphasize personal autonomy and self-determination, fostering entrepreneurship and artistic expression. Yet this approach can lead to social fragmentation and decreased sense of community belonging. Recent research in social psychology reveals that neither extreme produces optimal outcomes. Communities that thrive are those that skillfully balance these competing values. They create structures that protect individual rights while maintaining social bonds through shared values and mutual support. This balance is not static but requires continuous negotiation and adjustment as societies evolve. Countries that have successfully implemented this equilibrium demonstrate higher levels of citizen satisfaction, stronger social networks, and greater economic resilience. Therefore, the challenge for modern societies is not to choose between individual freedom and collective responsibility, but to ___________ them in ways that benefit both personal fulfillment and community well-being.
✓ ① completely separate and prioritize
② skillfully integrate and harmonize
③ gradually eliminate one of[하나를 제거하는 것이 아니라 둘 다를 유지해야 함이 명시되어 있습니다.]
④ alternately emphasize and diminish[지문은 정적인 선택이 아닌 지속적인 협상을 강조합니다.]
⑤ artificially impose regulations on[인공적인 규제보다는 자연스러운 균형을 추구해야 합니다.]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 개인의 자유와 집단의 책임 사이의 균형이 중요하며, 성공한 사회들이 '이 두 가치를 능숙하게 균형 맞춘다'고 설명합니다. 따라서 '② skillfully integrate and harmonize'가 가장 적절한 답입니다.
R-0045 11번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Throughout history, humans have sought to understand the natural world through observation and experimentation. The scientific method emerged as a powerful tool for acquiring knowledge, emphasizing the importance of testing hypotheses through controlled experiments. However, this approach has a significant limitation: it can only investigate phenomena that are ___________ and measurable. Aspects of human experience such as personal values, emotional depth, and spiritual beliefs fall outside the scope of scientific inquiry. This does not mean these dimensions are less important or valid; rather, it suggests that different methods of understanding are needed to explore them fully. Art, philosophy, and literature offer alternative pathways for examining the human condition, providing insights that complement scientific knowledge. The integration of these diverse perspectives creates a more complete picture of reality.
✓ ① observable
② theoretical
③ subjective
④ abstract
⑤ hypothetical
▶ 해설 보기
과학적 방법은 제어된 실험을 통해 가설을 검증하는 것을 강조하므로, 과학이 조사할 수 있는 현상은 '관찰 가능한(observable)'이어야 한다. 뒤에 'measurable'과 함께 나오는 표현으로 보아, 과학적 탐구의 대상이 되려면 관찰 가능해야 한다는 의미가 가장 자연스럽다.
R-0046 12번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Successful entrepreneurs often share a common characteristic: the ability to ___________ failure and learn from their mistakes. Rather than viewing setbacks as permanent defeats, they see them as valuable learning opportunities. This mindset transforms challenges into catalysts for growth and improvement. Many famous business leaders, including Steve Jobs and Oprah Winfrey, experienced significant failures before achieving tremendous success. They did not allow initial disappointments to discourage them; instead, they analyzed what went wrong and adjusted their strategies accordingly. This resilience and adaptability distinguish successful individuals from those who give up easily. Organizations that cultivate a culture accepting failure as part of the learning process tend to innovate more effectively and develop stronger solutions to complex problems.
✓ ① embrace
② ignore
③ punish
④ hide
⑤ prevent
▶ 해설 보기
성공한 기업가들의 특징이 실패를 '수용하고 받아들이는(embrace)' 것이라는 의미이다. 뒤에 '소중한 학습의 기회'라는 표현과 'setbacks'을 'catalyst for growth'로 본다는 점에서 embrace가 가장 적절하다.
R-0047 13번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The rise of social media has fundamentally changed how people communicate and share information. While these platforms have created unprecedented opportunities for connection and expression, they have also introduced new challenges. One significant concern is the spread of misinformation, which can ___________ public opinion and influence decision-making at both personal and societal levels. False or misleading information spreads rapidly across networks, often faster than factual corrections can reach the same audience. This phenomenon has implications for democracy, public health, and social cohesion. To address this issue, media literacy education has become increasingly important, helping individuals develop critical thinking skills to evaluate information sources and assess credibility. Platforms themselves also bear responsibility for implementing fact-checking mechanisms and reducing the visibility of unverified claims.
✓ ① distort
② clarify
③ analyze
④ strengthen
⑤ document
▶ 해설 보기
'거짓된 정보가 공중 의견을 왜곡하고 의사결정에 영향을 미칠 수 있다'는 의미이므로 'distort(왜곡하다)'가 정답이다. misinformation과 misleading information이 공중 의견에 부정적 영향을 미친다는 문맥에서 distort가 가장 적절하다.
R-0178 14번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Climate change represents one of the most pressing challenges facing humanity today. Rising global temperatures have triggered numerous environmental consequences, from melting polar ice caps to increasingly severe weather patterns. Addressing this crisis requires ___________ action at both individual and governmental levels. While individual efforts such as reducing carbon footprint and supporting sustainable products are important, they alone cannot solve a problem of this magnitude. Governments must implement comprehensive policies that regulate industrial emissions, invest in renewable energy sources, and protect natural ecosystems. International cooperation is equally vital, as climate change is a global phenomenon that transcends national boundaries. Only through coordinated and sustained efforts can we hope to mitigate the worst effects of climate change and secure a livable planet for future generations.
✓ ① coordinated
② temporary
③ voluntary
④ limited
⑤ gradual
▶ 해설 보기
문맥에서 개인적 노력만으로는 부족하며, 정부와 국제 협력이 필요하다는 점을 고려할 때, '조정된, 협력적인(coordinated)' 행동이 필요하다는 의미가 가장 적절하다. 마지막 문장의 'coordinated and sustained efforts'에서도 같은 단어가 반복되어 답이 확실하다.

R-0188 23번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
In economics, the concept of opportunity cost refers to the value of the next best alternative that must be foregone when making a choice. For example, if a student decides to attend university, the opportunity cost includes not only tuition fees but also the wages they could have earned by working instead. This principle reveals a fundamental truth about decision-making: every choice involves a trade-off. Interestingly, many people fail to consider opportunity costs in their daily decisions, leading to suboptimal outcomes. Research in behavioral economics shows that individuals tend to focus on explicit costs while ignoring implicit costs. A business executive, for instance, might invest in a project because it generates profit, without fully recognizing that the same capital could have been invested elsewhere with potentially higher returns. Understanding opportunity cost is crucial because ___________ enables us to make more rational and efficient decisions that maximize our overall well-being and resources.
✓ ①recognizing all alternatives and their associated values
②prioritizing short-term gains over long-term stability
③focusing primarily on explicit monetary costs
④relying on intuition rather than analytical thinking
⑤minimizing the total amount of available resources
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 주제는 기회비용의 개념과 그것을 이해하는 것의 중요성입니다. 지문에서 '사람들은 명시적 비용에만 집중하고 암묵적 비용을 무시한다'고 언급하며, 이것이 최적이 아닌 결정으로 이어진다고 설명합니다. 빈칸은 '이해하는 것이 더 합리적이고 효율적인 결정을 가능하게 한다'는 내용 앞에 위치합니다. 따라서 정답은 '모든 대안과 그 관련 가치를 인식하는 것(①recognizing all alternatives and their associated values)'으로, 이는 기회비용을 완전히 이해하는 핵심입니다.

R-0192 27번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Human memory is not a perfect recording device but rather a ___________ system that constantly reconstructs past experiences based on current knowledge and beliefs. When we retrieve a memory, we are not accessing a fixed file in our brain; instead, we are piecing together fragments of information and filling in gaps with plausible details. This reconstructive nature explains why eyewitness testimonies often contain inaccuracies despite the witness's conviction of their accuracy. Research shows that misleading information presented after an event can permanently alter how we remember that event. Our memories are shaped not only by what actually happened but also by our emotions, expectations, and subsequent experiences. Understanding this dynamic process is crucial for legal systems that rely on eyewitness accounts, as it highlights the limitations of human memory and the need for additional corroborating evidence.
✓ ①mechanical and unchangeable
②reconstructive and dynamic
③literal and objective
④isolated and independent
⑤sequential and linear
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 '메모리가 고정된 기록이 아니라 현재의 지식과 신념에 따라 과거 경험을 끊임없이 재구성하는 체계'라는 점입니다. 첫 번째 문장에서 'constantly reconstructs'라는 표현과 이후의 'piecing together fragments'와 'filling in gaps' 등의 설명이 메모리의 동적이고 재구성적 특성을 강조합니다. ②번 'reconstructive and dynamic(재구성적이고 동적인)'이 이러한 메모리의 본질을 가장 정확하게 표현합니다.
R-0193 28번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
In economic theory, the concept of opportunity cost is ___________ for understanding rational decision-making. Opportunity cost refers to the value of the best alternative foregone when making a choice. For instance, if you choose to spend three hours studying for an exam rather than working at a part-time job that pays fifteen dollars per hour, the opportunity cost of studying is forty-five dollars. This concept reveals that every decision involves a trade-off, and rational actors must compare the benefits of their chosen action against the benefits of alternative actions they must give up. Without considering opportunity costs, individuals and businesses may make decisions that appear beneficial in isolation but actually represent poor choices when all alternatives are weighed. This principle applies universally, from personal finance decisions to major corporate strategies, making it indispensable for sound economic reasoning.
✓ ①optional and subjective
②fundamental and essential
③complicated and controversial
④outdated and impractical
⑤supplementary and minor
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 경제적 의사결정에서 기회비용의 중요성을 설명하고 있습니다. '기회비용을 고려하지 않으면 잘못된 선택을 할 수 있다'는 내용과 '모든 결정에서 핵심적인 역할을 한다'는 주장이 이어집니다. 마지막 문장의 'indispensable for sound economic reasoning(건전한 경제적 추론에 필수불가결하다)'가 정답의 핵심입니다. ②번 'fundamental and essential(근본적이고 본질적인)'이 가장 적절합니다.
R-0194 29번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The process of photosynthesis demonstrates how plants ___________ solar energy into chemical energy that can be stored and used for growth and metabolism. During this remarkable transformation, chlorophyll molecules in plant leaves capture photons from sunlight, exciting electrons to higher energy states. This energy drives a series of chemical reactions that ultimately produce glucose and oxygen from carbon dioxide and water. The efficiency of this process varies across different plant species, depending on their evolutionary adaptations to specific environments. C3 plants, common in temperate regions, operate differently from C4 and CAM plants found in arid climates. Understanding photosynthesis is not merely an academic exercise; it has profound implications for agriculture, biofuel development, and climate change mitigation. As we face the challenge of feeding a growing population while reducing carbon emissions, optimizing photosynthetic efficiency could become increasingly important for human survival.
✓ ①distribute
②convert
③absorb
④reflect
⑤transmit
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 식물이 태양에너지를 어떤 형태로 변환하는지 설명하는 것입니다. 문맥에서 'solar energy into chemical energy(태양에너지를 화학에너지로 ___)'라는 표현이 있으며, 이는 에너지의 형태 변환을 나타냅니다. ②번 'convert(변환하다)'가 정답입니다. 'distribute(분배)', 'absorb(흡수)', 'reflect(반사)', 'transmit(전송)'은 광합성의 과정을 설명하기에 부정확합니다.
R-0195 30번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Throughout history, social hierarchies have often been ___________ through various mechanisms that perpetuate inequality across generations. One prominent mechanism is the educational system, which, despite its idealistic mission to provide equal opportunities, frequently reinforces existing class divisions. Children from wealthy families have access to better schools, tutoring, and extracurricular opportunities, giving them significant advantages over their less privileged peers. Another mechanism is the concentration of inherited wealth, which allows privileged families to maintain their economic status without requiring their members to work or excel. Cultural capital—the knowledge, behaviors, and tastes valued by the dominant group—is also transmitted within families, further advantages those born into the upper classes. Sociologists argue that recognizing these mechanisms is the first step toward creating genuinely meritocratic societies where individual talent and effort, rather than family background, determine success. Only by intentionally dismantling these structures can we hope to achieve true social mobility.
①challenged and reduced
✓ ①reformed and improved
③reproduced and maintained
④documented and analyzed
⑤created and expanded
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심 주장은 '사회 계층이 다양한 메커니즘을 통해 세대에 걸쳐 불평등을 지속시킨다'는 것입니다. 이어지는 문장들에서 교육 시스템, 상속된 부의 집중, 문화자본 등이 어떻게 기존의 불평등을 유지하고 재생산하는지 설명합니다. 특히 '이 구조를 의도적으로 해체해야만 한다'는 마지막 문장은 현재의 불평등이 계속 유지되고 있음을 시사합니다. ③번 'reproduced and maintained(재생산되고 유지되다)'가 정답입니다.
R-0196 31번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Stress is an organism's response to a stressor, and when humans experience stress, two major physiological systems activate. The sympathetic nervous system triggers the fight-or-flight response, releasing adrenaline to prepare the body for immediate action. However, this heightened state cannot be sustained indefinitely. The parasympathetic nervous system must then ___________ in order to restore the body to its normal state of equilibrium. Meanwhile, the HPA axis regulates cortisol release, which influences metabolic and immunological functions. When stress becomes chronic, these systems remain constantly activated, leading to increased susceptibility to disease and mental health disorders. Understanding these mechanisms is crucial for developing effective stress management strategies.
①maximize the intensity of the stress response
✓ ②gradually activate emergency hormones
③return the body to homeostasis
④suppress emotional reactions entirely
⑤enhance memory and cognitive function
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 스트레스 대응의 이중 체계입니다. 교감신경계가 fight-or-flight 반응을 일으킨 후, '이 상태가 무한정 지속될 수 없다'는 점이 강조됩니다. 따라서 부교감신경계는 신체를 정상 상태(equilibrium/homeostasis)로 돌려놓아야 합니다. 정답은 '③return the body to homeostasis'입니다.
R-0197 32번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Seed banks represent a revolutionary approach to preserving agricultural heritage and ensuring food security. These facilities function as ___________ against the permanent loss of plant genetic diversity caused by climate change, disease, or natural disasters. As environmental conditions shift rapidly due to global warming, scientists recognize that the genetic variations preserved in these vaults will be indispensable for developing crop varieties adapted to drought, heat, and emerging pathogens. Without such repositories, humanity would lose irreplaceable agricultural resources accumulated over centuries of selective breeding.
✓ ①temporary solutions to agricultural market fluctuations
②biological insurance policies ensuring genetic diversity preservation
③methods for increasing crop yields immediately
④barriers preventing international agricultural trade
⑤technologies for controlling global climate patterns
▶ 해설 보기
지문에서 '식물 유전 다양성의 영구적 손실에 맞서는 역할'을 강조하며, 기후변화와 재해로부터 보호한다는 점이 명시됩니다. 정답은 '②biological insurance policies ensuring genetic diversity preservation'입니다. 이는 '보험'이라는 개념으로 미래의 위험에 대비하면서 유전적 다양성을 보존한다는 지문의 핵심 주장과 정확히 일치합니다.
R-0198 33번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Economic anthropology reveals how different cultures develop distinct systems of exchange and value creation. In many traditional societies, the gift economy operates on fundamentally different principles than market economies. Rather than ___________ through abstract monetary transactions, gift economies build social relationships through the reciprocal exchange of goods and services over time. When someone gives a gift, they create a social obligation for the recipient to reciprocate, establishing bonds of interdependence that strengthen community ties. This system persists in modern societies in various forms: friendships are maintained through birthday gifts, families exchange presents during holidays, and professional networks are built through favors and mutual support. Anthropologists argue that understanding gift economies is crucial for recognizing that economic behavior is not universally driven by profit maximization but is deeply embedded in cultural values and social structures.
①measuring value through kinship networks
✓ ②quantifying relationships through material accumulation
③assigning value primarily through monetary exchange
④creating artificial scarcity of resources
⑤establishing hierarchical power structures
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 선물 경제와 시장 경제의 대조를 통해 핵심 주장을 전개합니다. 선물 경제에서는 추상적인 금전 거래가 아니라 시간에 걸친 상호 교환으로 사회적 관계를 구축한다고 명시합니다. 정답은 '③assigning value primarily through monetary exchange'입니다. 이는 선물 경제가 이를 '하지 않는다'는 대조를 통해 지문의 핵심을 명확히 합니다.
R-0242 34번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The development of written language fundamentally transformed human cognition and social organization. Before writing emerged, human knowledge was transmitted orally through storytelling, songs, and memorization techniques passed down across generations. However, the invention of writing systems allowed information to be ___________ independent of individual memory, enabling the accumulation of knowledge across centuries. This external storage of information freed cognitive resources previously dedicated to memorization, allowing humans to engage in more complex abstract thinking and reasoning. The emergence of written records also facilitated the rise of large-scale institutions, standardized laws, and centralized governments, as administrative systems could now track resources and populations systematically. Scholars argue that the cognitive revolution brought by writing extends beyond mere record-keeping; it fundamentally altered how humans think, reason, and organize society itself.
✓ ①transmitted exclusively through individual cognition
②stored externally, independent of individual memory
③transmitted orally within family units only
④encoded secretly for governmental purposes
⑤simplified to accommodate limited literacy
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 문자의 혁명적 역할입니다. "이전에는 개인의 기억에 의존했지만, 문자의 출현으로 인해" 정보가 "개인의 기억과 무관하게" 저장될 수 있게 되었다는 점이 강조됩니다. 이를 통해 인지 자원이 해방되고 더 복잡한 사고가 가능해졌습니다. 정답은 '②stored externally, independent of individual memory'입니다.
R-0199 35번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Recent studies in cognitive psychology reveal that human memory is not a perfect recording device but rather a ___________ system that reconstructs past events based on current knowledge and beliefs. When we recall an event, we do not simply retrieve stored information; instead, we actively rebuild the memory by filling in gaps with plausible details. This reconstructive process can lead to distortions where we unconsciously alter details to fit our current understanding or emotional state. Research on eyewitness testimony demonstrates this phenomenon vividly. Witnesses often report seeing things that were never actually present, influenced by suggestions from investigators or their own expectations. Understanding memory as reconstructive rather than reproductive has profound implications for how we evaluate evidence in legal proceedings and how we understand the reliability of personal recollections.
✓ ①mechanical
②reconstructive
③archival
④photographic
⑤sequential
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 인간의 기억이 정확한 기록이 아니라 현재의 지식과 신념에 기초하여 과거 사건을 '다시 구성하는' 시스템이라는 것입니다. 지문 전체에서 'reconstruct', 'reconstructive', 'rebuild'와 같은 단어들이 반복되며, 기억이 단순히 저장된 정보를 꺼내는 것이 아니라 능동적으로 기억을 재구성한다고 설명합니다. 따라서 정답은 ②reconstructive입니다.
R-0234 36번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
In evolutionary biology, the concept of convergent evolution demonstrates that different species facing similar environmental pressures often develop ___________ solutions to the same problems. For example, the wings of birds, bats, and insects evolved independently from different ancestral structures, yet they serve the same function of flight. Similarly, the eye has evolved separately in octopuses and vertebrates, resulting in remarkably similar sensory organs despite completely different evolutionary paths. This phenomenon suggests that natural selection can arrive at comparable solutions when organisms encounter identical selective pressures. However, convergent evolution is not inevitable—the specific form that a solution takes depends on the particular constraints and opportunities provided by each species' genetic and developmental heritage. Understanding convergent evolution provides evidence for the power of natural selection and reveals the principles underlying the organization of life on Earth.
①different
✓ ②redundant
③analogous
④primitive
⑤transitional
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 수렴진화(convergent evolution)에서 비슷한 환경 압력에 직면한 다양한 종들이 같은 문제에 대해 '유사한/같은 종류의' 해결책을 개발한다는 것입니다. 새, 박쥐, 곤충의 날개와 문어와 척추동물의 눈이 독립적으로 진화했지만 놀랍도록 유사한 구조를 갖는다는 예시가 이를 보여줍니다. 따라서 정답은 ③analogous (유사한)입니다. analogous는 '본질적으로 다르지만 기능이나 구조가 유사한'을 의미합니다.
R-0200 37번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Economic theories of consumer behavior have traditionally assumed that individuals make purchasing decisions based on rational cost-benefit analysis. However, behavioral economics has revealed that consumers frequently make choices that ___________ this rational model. For instance, people often pay premium prices for branded products despite identical functionality in generic alternatives, driven by psychological factors like brand loyalty and social status concerns. The 'endowment effect' demonstrates that people value items more highly simply because they own them, regardless of objective market value. Additionally, temporal factors influence decision-making in ways pure rationality cannot explain; consumers may choose immediate gratification over delayed but greater rewards. These findings suggest that emotions, social influences, and cognitive biases play roles equal to or exceeding that of rational calculation. Marketing professionals have leveraged these insights to create strategies that appeal to non-rational aspects of human psychology.
①confirm
✓ ②reinforce
③deviate from
④exemplify
⑤strengthen
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심 주장은 전통적 경제이론의 이성적 소비자 모델과 달리, 행동경제학이 소비자들이 실제로는 이 합리적 모델로부터 '벗어난' 선택을 자주 한다는 것을 밝혀냈다는 것입니다. 브랜드 프리미엄, 보유 효과, 시간적 편향 등의 예시들이 모두 합리적 모델에서의 편차를 보여줍니다. 따라서 정답은 ③deviate from (벗어나다)입니다.
R-0201 38번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Philosophers of mind have long grappled with the 'hard problem of consciousness'—explaining why subjective experiences feel the way they do. While neuroscientists can map brain activity and explain cognitive functions, they struggle to explain why seeing the color red produces a specific subjective experience. This explanatory gap between physical processes and conscious experience has led some philosophers to argue that consciousness may be ___________ reducible to purely physical mechanisms. Others maintain that our current scientific frameworks are simply inadequate, and that consciousness will eventually be explained through advances in neurobiology. The debate hinges on whether consciousness is fundamentally different in kind from other physical phenomena or merely different in complexity. Recent research in neurophenomenology attempts to bridge this gap by carefully examining both neural correlates and first-person accounts of experience, suggesting that consciousness may emerge from the interaction between brain processes and bodily states rather than being exclusively located in neural activity.
✓ ①fully
②not entirely
③readily
④necessarily
⑤ultimately
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 의식의 '하드 문제'를 다루는 것으로, 신경과학적 설명과 주관적 경험 사이의 설명 간격(explanatory gap)이 존재한다는 것입니다. 일부 철학자들은 의식이 순수하게 물리적 메커니즘으로 '완전히' 환원될 수 없다고 주장한다는 내용입니다. 지문 후반의 신경현상학적 연구가 의식이 뇌 활동뿐만 아니라 신체 상태와의 상호작용에서 나타날 수 있다고 제시하면서, 의식이 '완전히는 아니지만' 물리적 메커니즘으로 설명될 수 있음을 시사합니다. 따라서 정답은 ②not entirely (완전히는 아니다)입니다.
R-0202 39번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The bandwagon effect describes a psychological phenomenon wherein individuals adopt beliefs or behaviors primarily because many others have done so. The term itself has interesting historical roots. A literal 'bandwagon' is a wagon carrying musicians during parades or circus events. The phrase 'jump on the bandwagon' emerged during Zachary Taylor's 1848 presidential campaign when a famous circus clown invited Taylor to ride his bandwagon. As Taylor gained popularity, opponents were mockingly told they should also 'jump on the bandwagon' if they wanted to associate with success. Later, during William Jennings Bryan's 1900 campaign, this phrase became standard political language, though it took on a ___________. Despite its 19th-century origins, the theoretical foundation of the bandwagon effect was only rigorously studied in the 1950s through Asch's conformity experiments, which revealed significant individual variations in susceptibility to social pressure.
✓ ①positive and enthusiastic connotation
②derogatory meaning, implying superficial conformity without critical thought
③neutral and purely descriptive meaning
④technical psychological definition
⑤literal reference to actual wagons
▶ 해설 보기
지문에서 '1900년 Bryan 캠프 이후 이 표현이 standard political language가 되었지만'이라는 문장에서 변화가 있음을 암시합니다. 뒤의 설명 'implying that people were associating themselves with success without considering'이 직접적으로 그 변화의 내용을 보여줍니다. 즉, 원래는 단순히 인기에 편승한다는 의미였으나, 시간이 지나면서 '비판적 사고 없이 성공에만 편승하는 것을 조롱하는' 부정적 의미(derogatory meaning)로 변화했다는 것입니다. 따라서 정답은 ②번입니다.
R-0203 40번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Consumer behavior research has long examined how individuals make purchasing decisions, yet recent studies reveal that external social cues significantly influence choices in ways consumers often fail to recognize. When shoppers observe that a product is bestselling or heavily purchased by others, they tend to perceive it as higher quality and more desirable, regardless of its actual merit. This phenomenon extends beyond retail environments. In restaurants, items labeled 'most popular' attract more orders not necessarily because they are superior, but because of ___________. Restaurant owners have exploited this tendency strategically, using menu design and marketing language to guide customer choices toward higher-margin items. Similarly, streaming platforms prominently display 'trending' content, which generates further viewership and creates a self-reinforcing cycle. Critics contend that such practices manipulate consumer autonomy by leveraging psychological biases rather than allowing informed decision-making. Understanding these mechanisms is crucial for consumers who wish to make choices based on genuine preferences rather than social signals that may be artificially amplified.
✓ ①the superior nutritional content of popular dishes
②the influence of social proof and the assumption that popular choices reflect quality
③the effort required to read the entire menu
④the expertise of the restaurant's chef
⑤the lower prices of best-selling items
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 핵심은 소비자들이 객관적 품질보다 '사회적 신호'에 의해 영향을 받는다는 것입니다. 첫 번째 예시에서 '베스트셀러'로 보이는 제품이 실제 가치와 무관하게 선택된다는 점을 강조합니다. 음식점 예시에서도 'most popular'라고 표시된 메뉴 항목이 더 많이 주문되는 이유는 '더 좋아서가 아니라'라는 구절이 나옵니다. 뒤의 문장들에서 '심리적 편향을 활용'한다고 명시하고, 마지막에 '인공적으로 증폭된 사회 신호'를 언급합니다. 따라서 정답은 '사회적 증거(social proof)와 인기 있는 선택이 품질을 반영한다는 가정'을 의미하는 ②번입니다.
R-0204 41번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The practice of keeping detailed records and documentation has transformed human civilization in profound ways. Before the development of writing systems, knowledge was transmitted orally, limiting how much information could be preserved and communicated across generations. The emergence of writing, particularly in the form of administrative records in ancient Mesopotamia, enabled societies to maintain complex bureaucracies, trade networks, and legal systems that would have been impossible to manage through memory alone. Documentation established a mechanism by which ___________. Written records allowed societies to impose consistent standards, hold individuals accountable, and build institutional knowledge that transcended any single person's lifespan. As civilizations developed more sophisticated forms of record-keeping, from clay tablets to printing presses to digital databases, the capacity for societal organization increased exponentially. Historians recognize that the shift from oral to literate cultures marked a fundamental turning point in human development, enabling not only technological advancement but also the development of specialized knowledge systems such as science, law, and medicine. Today, in an era of unprecedented data collection, the power and implications of record-keeping remain as significant as ever.
✓ ①oral traditions could be more efficiently preserved than written texts
①knowledge could be standardized, preserved, and made accountable across time and populations
③human memory became obsolete in managing complex societies
④writing systems replaced all forms of social organization
⑤oral communication lost its cultural significance entirely
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 기록의 발전이 인간 문명에 어떤 영향을 미쳤는지를 설명합니다. 특히 문어 이전에는 구두 전승만 존재했고, 이는 정보 보존에 제한이 있었다고 말합니다. 빈칸 이후의 문장에서 '기록은 사회가 일관된 기준을 부과하고 개인에게 책임을 물으며 어떤 개인의 수명을 초월하는 제도적 지식을 구축할 수 있게 했다'고 명시합니다. 이는 기록이 지식을 '표준화하고, 보존하며, 시간과 인구 전체에 걸쳐 책임질 수 있게 만들었다'는 의미입니다. 따라서 정답은 ②번입니다.
R-0205 42번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Mnemonic devices are learning techniques that aid information retention by associating new information with something easier to remember. They utilize elaborative encoding, retrieval cues, and imagery to encode information efficiently. The human mind naturally remembers spatial, personal, surprising, and humorous information more readily than abstract forms. Ancient Greeks and Romans recognized this distinction, categorizing memory into two types: natural memory, which is innate and instinctive, and artificial memory, which ___________. Mnemonic systems are consciously employed strategies that leverage information already stored in long-term memory, making the memorization process significantly more efficient and effective for learners of all ages.
✓ ①must be developed through deliberate training and practice
②operates independently of conscious effort
③cannot be improved through systematic methods
④is primarily useful for memorizing abstract concepts
⑤depends exclusively on individual genetic factors
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 자연적 기억(natural memory)과 인공적 기억(artificial memory)의 차이를 설명합니다. 자연적 기억은 타고난 것이고 본능적으로 사용되는 반면, 인공적 기억은 이와 대조적으로(in contrast) 학습과 연습을 통해 훈련되고 발전되어야 한다고 명시되어 있습니다. 따라서 정답은 ①'의도적인 훈련과 연습을 통해 발전되어야 한다'입니다.
R-0206 43번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
In Conan Doyle's narrative, Sherlock Holmes maintains an unusual relationship with Irene Adler, referring to her as 'the woman' among all others. However, his feelings toward her are not rooted in romantic love, which he considers incompatible with his analytical nature. Holmes believes that emotions, particularly love, would compromise his reasoning abilities. He views strong feelings as distracting factors that could ___________. To his logically trained mind, the introduction of such emotional elements would be as detrimental as introducing physical defects into a precision instrument. Yet paradoxically, despite his conscious rejection of emotional attachment, Irene Adler remains the sole woman of significance in his extraordinary life, a testament to the complexity of human psychology.
✓ ①enhance his capacity for systematic observation
②undermine the reliability of his mental conclusions
③strengthen his ability to understand criminal behavior
④improve his deductive reasoning techniques
⑤expand his emotional intelligence and empathy
▶ 해설 보기
지문에서 Holmes는 감정이 '그의 정신적 결과의 신뢰성에 의문을 던질 수 있는 산만한 요소'라고 표현합니다. 원문에 '의심을 던질 수 있는 산만한 요소(distracting factor which might throw a doubt upon all his mental results)'라고 명시되어 있으므로, 정답은 ②'그의 정신적 결론의 신뢰성을 훼손할 수 있다'입니다.
R-0085 44번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Memory is not a perfect recording device that stores experiences exactly as they occur. Rather, it is a reconstructive process where the brain actively assembles information based on existing knowledge and current context. When we recall a past event, we don't retrieve a fixed file; instead, we ___________. This explains why eyewitness accounts often contain inaccuracies despite witnesses' confidence. The brain fills in missing details with plausible information, creating a coherent narrative that feels authentic. Studies show that repeated questioning or exposure to misleading information can alter original memories. Understanding this reconstructive nature of memory has important implications for legal proceedings and historical documentation.
✓ ① recreate the memory using patterns and schemas we've developed
② store the exact details in our neural networks permanently
③ forget most experiences within a few hours
④ rely entirely on what other people tell us about events
⑤ maintain perfect accuracy of all sensory information
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 기억이 '재구성 과정(reconstructive process)'임을 핵심 주장으로 제시합니다. 첫 문장에서 '완벽한 기록장치가 아니라'고 부정하고, 'actively assembles information'이라고 설명합니다. 빈칸 앞의 'we don't retrieve a fixed file; instead, we'는 대조 구조로, 기억이 '고정된 파일이 아니라 능동적으로 구성된다'는 의미를 완성해야 합니다. ①번 'recreate the memory using patterns and schemas'는 '기존 지식(existing knowledge)을 활용해 기억을 재창조한다'는 지문의 핵심을 정확히 환언합니다. ②는 'permanently store'로 재구성 개념에 모순, ③④⑤는 지문과 무관한 오답입니다.
R-0086 45번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Creativity is often romanticized as a spontaneous burst of inspiration that cannot be taught or trained. However, brain research research reveals a different picture. The creative process involves specific cognitive mechanisms: pattern recognition, remote association*, and cognitive flexibility. Individuals who excel at creativity don't simply wait for inspiration; they ___________. Regular practice in problem-solving, exposure to diverse fields, and deliberate experimentation strengthen neural networks associated with creative thinking. Studies of accomplished artists and innovators show that their creative breakthroughs typically follow periods of intense focused work. This suggests that creativity, like athletics or music, can be developed through systematic training and environmental support.
✓ ① abandon logical thinking in favor of pure intuition
② engage in sustained practice and deliberate experimentation
③ rely exclusively on innate genetic predisposition
④ avoid exposure to other people's ideas and influences
⑤ reproduce existing patterns without modification
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 '창의성은 가르칠 수 없는 영감이라는 통념을 반박한다'는 'However'로 시작합니다. 창의성이 '특정한 인지 메커니즘'을 포함하고, 훈련을 통해 개발될 수 있음을 주장합니다. 'don't simply wait for inspiration; they'는 창의적 인물들의 적극적 행동을 설명해야 하며, 그 직후 'Regular practice in problem-solving... strengthen neural networks'가 구체적 근거로 제시됩니다. ②번 'engage in sustained practice and deliberate experimentation'은 '지속적인 연습과 의도적인 실험에 참여한다'는 의미로 'Regular practice'와 'deliberate experimentation'을 정확히 환언합니다. ①③④⑤는 지문의 '체계적 훈련' 논지와 모순됩니다.
R-0087 46번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Social norms are the implicit rules that govern behavior within communities and societies. They are maintained not through formal laws but through subtle mechanisms of social approval and disapproval. Individuals who conform to norms receive social acceptance and trust, while those who deviate face criticism or exclusion. In addition, ___________. People internalize social norms during childhood and adolescence through observation and reinforcement, developing an intrinsic motivation to follow them. This absorption explains why people often follow norms even when external enforcement is absent. However, norms are not static; they evolve as societies change and new generations challenge established expectations. Understanding this dynamic nature of norms is essential for social change.
✓ ① norms are purely conscious decisions made by individuals
② social enforcement mechanisms ensure compliance through internalized values
③ most people are aware of all the norms they follow
④ norms prevent all forms of social change and progress
⑤ deviation from norms is always beneficial to society
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 '사회규범이 공식 법률이 아닌 사회적 승인/불승인으로 유지된다'고 설명합니다. 'In addition,'은 추가 설명 부분을 도입하며, '사람들이 아동기에 규범을 내재화한다'는 내용이 따라옵니다. 빈칸은 '사회 강제 메커니즘'과 '내재화된 동기'의 연결 고리를 설명해야 합니다. ②번 'social enforcement mechanisms ensure compliance through internalized values'는 '사회적 강제 메커니즘이 내재화된 가치를 통해 준수를 보장한다'는 의미로, 지문의 '외부 강제 없이도 규범을 따르는 이유'를 정확히 환언합니다. ①③④⑤는 지문의 '내재화' 메커니즘을 설명하지 못합니다.
R-0088 47번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Photosynthesis is the biochemical process by which plants convert light energy into chemical energy stored in glucose molecules. This process occurs in two main stages: light-dependent reactions in the thylakoid membrane* and light-independent reactions in the stroma*. The efficiency of photosynthesis varies significantly across plant species and environmental conditions. However, ___________. A plant's light-absorbing rate depends on light intensity, carbon dioxide concentration, temperature, and water availability. When any of these factors becomes limiting, the overall rate decreases regardless of other favorable conditions. This principle, known as Liebig's Law of the Minimum*, explains why optimizing all factors is crucial for agricultural productivity and crop yield.
✓ ① most plants can perform photosynthesis in complete darkness
② a single limiting factor determines the overall photosynthetic rate
③ temperature has no effect on photosynthetic efficiency
④ photosynthesis requires no external energy sources
⑤ glucose is converted back into light energy immediately
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 '광합성 효율이 종(種)과 환경 조건에 따라 다르다'는 주장을 'However'로 반전시킵니다. 'A plant's light-absorbing rate depends on'으로 여러 요소를 나열하고, '이 요소 중 하나가 제한되면 전체 속도가 감소한다'고 설명합니다. 빈칸은 '효율의 차이'가 무엇 때문인지를 설명해야 하며, 바로 다음 문장에서 '제한 요소(limiting factor)' 개념으로 구체화됩니다. ②번 'a single limiting factor determines the overall light-absorbing rate'는 'Liebig's Law of the Minimum'을 정확히 환언하며, 지문의 핵심 논거입니다. ①③④⑤는 광합성의 기본 원리와 모순됩니다.
R-0089 48번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Memory is not a perfect recording device that stores experiences exactly as they occurred. Rather, it is a reconstructive process in which the brain actively shapes and modifies information based on existing knowledge and current beliefs. When we recall an event, we don't simply retrieve a stored file; instead, we ___________. This explains why eyewitness testimonies often contain errors despite the witness's confidence. Studies show that repeated questioning can distort memories, inserting false details that feel completely authentic to the person remembering. The implications are significant: our sense of the past is not a reliable mirror of what actually happened.
✓ ①literally reproduce the exact details of what occurred
②reconstruct the memory by filling gaps with plausible information
③prioritize emotional experiences over factual accuracy
④preserve memories in their original neurological form
⑤forget most events within a short period of time
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 기억이 단순 저장이 아닌 '재구성 과정'임을 주장합니다. 첫 문장의 'reconstructive process'와 '우리는 저장된 파일을 단순히 검색하지 않는다'는 표현으로부터, 빈칸은 그 대신 무엇을 하는지 설명해야 합니다. ②번 'reconstruct the memory by filling gaps with plausible information'은 '틈을 타당한 정보로 채워서 기억을 재구성한다'는 의미로, 지문의 'reconstructive process'와 'brain actively shapes and modifies'를 정확히 환언합니다. ①은 정반대(완벽 재현), ③은 부분적 정확성만 있음, ④⑤는 지문 논지와 무관합니다.
R-0090 49번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
In traditional economies, the value of a product was determined primarily by its production costs—labor, materials, and overhead. However, modern consumer markets operate on a fundamentally different principle. Today, price often reflects not what something costs to make, but ___________. A smartphone might have $200 in production costs yet sell for $800, with the difference accounting for brand recognition, perceived quality, and consumer desire. This shift has led companies to invest heavily in marketing and design rather than merely optimizing manufacturing efficiency. Understanding this transformation is essential for comprehending contemporary capitalism.
✓ ①the efficiency of the manufacturing process employed
②what consumers believe the product is worth to them
③the scarcity of raw materials in global markets
④government regulations and tax policies
⑤the wages paid to factory workers
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 'However'로 전통경제와 현대 시장의 근본적 차이를 제시합니다. 전자는 '생산비용'이, 후자는 '생산비용이 아닌 다른 무언가'로 가격이 결정된다고 합니다. 스마트폰 예시에서 $200의 생산비용 vs $800의 판매가는 '브랜드, 인지된 품질, 소비자 욕구'의 차이라고 설명합니다. ②번 'what consumers believe the product is worth to them'은 '소비자가 그 제품을 자신에게 얼마나 가치 있다고 생각하는가'로, 지문의 주장을 정확히 환언합니다. ①③④⑤는 모두 생산 관련 요소들이거나 지문의 논점과 맞지 않습니다.
R-0091 50번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The phenomenon of "social proof*" plays a crucial role in shaping human behavior. When individuals observe others engaging in a particular action, they are more likely to imitate that behavior, often without conscious deliberation. This occurs because people tend to assume that if many others are doing something, it must be correct or beneficial. In addition, this mechanism is especially powerful in ambiguous situations where people feel uncertain about the right course of action. Rather than carefully evaluating available information, they ___________. Advertisers and political campaigners exploit this bias by showcasing how many people already support their product or candidate, leveraging our natural inclination toward conformity.
✓ ①rely on independent critical analysis of the situation
①follow the apparent consensus of the group
③demand objective evidence before making decisions
④question the motives of the majority around them
⑤develop stronger individual opinions in response
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 'social proof'의 메커니즘을 설명하며, 불확실한 상황에서 사람들이 '어떻게 하는가'를 설명해야 합니다. 'Rather than carefully evaluating available information'(신중한 평가 대신)이라는 구조로 대조되는 행동을 찾아야 합니다. ②번 'follow the apparent consensus of the group'은 '그룹의 명백한 합의를 따른다'는 의미로, 지문의 '사람들이 다른 사람들을 모방한다'는 주장과 '우리의 동조 성향'을 정확히 반영합니다. ①③④⑤는 모두 신중한 분석이나 비판적 평가를 시사하여 'Rather than' 이후 문맥에 맞지 않습니다. *social proof: 사회적 증거
R-0149 51번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The halo effect, a cognitive bias first identified by psychologist Edward Thorndike, describes how our overall impression of a person significantly influences how we evaluate their specific traits. For instance, if we perceive someone as physically attractive, we tend to assume they are also intelligent, trustworthy, and competent—despite lacking evidence for these assumptions. This phenomenon occurs because humans naturally seek cognitive efficiency; rather than carefully assessing each attribute independently, we ___________ by generalizing from one prominent characteristic to form judgments about unrelated qualities. The halo effect has profound implications in marketing, politics, and hiring practices, where initial positive impressions can disproportionately shape subsequent evaluations.
✓ ① take cognitive shortcuts that oversimplify our perception of people
② develop a systematic approach to understanding human behavior[②는 '체계적 접근'을 제시하는데, 지문은 오히려 체계성 없이 일반화하는 경향을 비판합니다]
③ conduct thorough investigations before making any judgments[③는 '철저한 조사'를 의미하나, 지문은 정반대로 신중한 평가를 하지 않는 경향을 설명합니]
④ prioritize factual evidence over subjective impressions[④는 '사실 증거 우선'을 강조하는데, 지문의 초점은 사람들이 증거 없이 가정한다는 것입니]
⑤ separate physical appearance from professional competence[⑤는 '분리'를 의미하지만, 지문은 오히려 부당한 '연결'이 일어난다는 것이 핵심입니다.]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 인지 편향으로서 할로 효과가 어떻게 작동하는지 설명합니다. '인지 효율성을 추구하기 때문에'라는 앞 문장에서 '각 속성을 독립적으로 신중히 평가하는 대신' 이어지는 빈칸에는 인간이 한 특성에서 다른 특성으로 일반화함으로써 어떻게 행동하는지를 묘사해야 합니다. ①번이 '인지적 지름길을 취함으로써 우리의 인식을 과도하게 단순화한다'는 의미로, 효율성 추구와 일반화 과정을 정확히 설명합니다.
R-0150 52번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The rewilding movement represents a paradigm shift in conservation strategy, moving beyond the traditional preservation model toward active ecological restoration. Rather than simply maintaining existing protected areas, rewilding initiatives reintroduce keystone species—organisms whose removal dramatically alters entire ecosystems—to restore natural processes and biodiversity. In the European rewilding project, wolves were reintroduced to regions where they had been extinct for centuries. Their return triggered a cascade of ecological changes: reduced deer populations allowed vegetation to recover, which stabilized riverbanks and improved water quality. This demonstrates that ___________ because the presence or absence of a single species can fundamentally reshape entire landscapes through interconnected biological relationships. Rewilding thus challenges the notion that conservation means keeping nature static.
① ecosystems should be completely isolated from human interference
✓ ② biodiversity requires constant human management and intervention
③ ecological systems are deeply interconnected and interdependent[②는 '지속적 인간 관리 필요'를 강조하는데, 지문은 자연 과정의 회복을 중심으로 논합니다]
④ predator species are inherently dangerous and should be avoided[④는 포식자의 위험성을 강조하지만, 지문은 늑대 재도입의 생태적 이점을 긍정적으로 설명합니]
⑤ conservation efforts should focus exclusively on protecting individual species[⑤는 '개별 종 보호'에 초점을 두는데, 지문의 핵심은 종 간의 상호작용과 시스템 전체입니]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 늑대 재도입이 사슴 개체수 감소 → 식생 회복 → 강둑 안정 → 수질 개선으로 이어지는 일련의 생태계 변화를 설명합니다. 빈칸 앞 문장 '한 종의 존재나 부재가 상호연결된 생물학적 관계를 통해 전체 경관을 근본적으로 재구성할 수 있다'는 내용과 연결되는 결론은 ③번 '생태계는 깊이 상호연결되고 상호의존적이다'입니다.
R-0151 53번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Metacognition—the ability to think about one's own thinking—has emerged as a critical factor in academic achievement and learning efficiency. Students who regularly monitor their comprehension, evaluate their study strategies, and adjust their learning methods demonstrate significantly higher performance than those who simply accumulate study time passively. Research shows that metacognitive awareness enables learners to identify knowledge gaps before exams and redirect their efforts toward weak areas. However, the relationship between metacognitive skill and academic success is not automatic. Many students possess metacognitive awareness yet fail to ___________ by continuing ineffective study habits despite recognizing their shortcomings. This paradox reveals that metacognition alone is insufficient; successful learning requires the motivational commitment to act on self-awareness and fundamentally alter established patterns.
✓ ① understand the importance of consistent practice and repetition
② implement the necessary changes based on their self-assessments[①은 '반복과 연습의 중요성'으로, 지문의 핵심인 '자기 인식을 행동으로 옮기기'와 다릅니]
③ develop a comprehensive theory of how learning works[③은 '학습 이론 개발'인데, 지문은 이론이 아닌 실제 행동 변화를 논합니다.]
④ increase the amount of time spent on studying each subject[④는 '공부 시간 증가'인데, 지문은 이미 '수동적으로 시간을 축적'하는 문제를 언급했으므]
⑤ collaborate with peers to share different learning techniques[⑤는 '동료와 협력'으로, 지문의 '개인의 자기 인식과 실행'이라는 초점과 무관합니다.]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 메타인지 능력이 있음에도 불구하고('despite recognizing their shortcomings') 학생들이 실패하는 역설을 설명합니다. 앞 문장에서 '지식 격차를 파악하고 노력을 약한 영역으로 돌린다'는 긍정적 사례를 제시한 후, 반전으로 많은 학생들이 '자신의 부족함을 인식하면서도' 실패한다고 합니다. 따라서 빈칸은 '자기 평가에 기반하여 필요한 변화를 실행하다'는 의미의 ②번이 정답입니다.
R-0152 54번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Artificial intelligence systems trained on large datasets can exhibit biases that reflect historical inequalities embedded in their training data. For example, an AI hiring algorithm trained on past recruitment records may perpetuate gender discrimination if those historical records show that certain positions were predominantly filled by men. The algorithm learns to replicate these patterns, not because it harbors prejudice, but because it ___________ by identifying statistical regularities without moral judgment. This phenomenon highlights a crucial distinction: AI systems lack the human capacity for ethical reasoning and normative evaluation. While humans can recognize unfair patterns and consciously choose to break them, machines simply optimize for accuracy on historical data. Consequently, deploying AI in high-stakes domains like hiring and criminal justice requires rigorous auditing and human oversight to prevent the technological amplification of social injustices.
✓ ① incorporates explicit rules programmed by human developers
② mechanically reproduces correlations found in past data[①은 '명시적 규칙 통합'인데, 지문은 오히려 프로그래밍된 규칙보다는 데이터로부터 학습하는]
③ attempts to understand the underlying causes of historical patterns[③은 '근본 원인 이해'인데, 지문은 AI가 원인을 이해하지 않고 상관관계만 재현한다고 명]
④ prioritizes fairness and equity in all its computational processes[④는 '공정성 우선'으로, 지문은 정반대로 AI가 윤리적 판단을 결여했다고 강조합니다.]
⑤ learns to challenge and question the validity of training datasets[⑤는 '데이터 유효성 문제 제기'인데, 기계는 이러한 비판적 질문을 할 능력이 없다는 것이]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 AI가 편향을 보이는 이유를 설명합니다. '도덕적 판단 없이 통계적 규칙성을 파악함으로써'라는 후속 절과 연결되려면, 빈칸은 AI가 '과거 데이터에서 발견된 상관관계를 기계적으로 재현한다'는 의미의 ②번이 정답입니다. 이는 AI의 도덕성 부재와 인간의 윤리적 판단 능력의 대조를 강조합니다.
R-0153 55번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The concept of 'psychological ownership' describes the feeling of possessiveness toward objects or spaces that one does not legally own. Renters often develop deep emotional attachments to their apartments despite having no formal ownership rights, while office workers may feel possessive about their desks and cubicles. Psychologists attribute this phenomenon to our innate human need for territorial control and identity expression. When individuals invest time, effort, or personal touches into a space—decorating, organizing, or customizing it—they psychologically claim it as 'theirs.' Interestingly, psychological ownership can ___________; studies show that tenants who invest in personalizing their rental spaces report higher satisfaction and better maintenance habits, yet they simultaneously experience greater distress when forced to relocate. This demonstrates that while psychological attachment can enhance immediate well-being and responsibility, it may also increase vulnerability to future disappointment and loss.
① reduce people's motivation to maintain shared spaces properly
✓ ② be entirely eliminated through education and awareness training
③ produce both positive and negative psychological consequences[②는 '교육으로 완전히 제거 가능'이라 하는데, 이는 '타고난 인간의 필요'라는 앞 문장과]
④ prevent individuals from forming new attachments to different places[④는 '새로운 장소 애착 방해'인데, 지문은 단순히 양면적 영향을 논하지, 새 애착 형성 ]
⑤ strengthen people's legal rights of ownership and property control[⑤는 '법적 소유권 강화'인데, 지문은 법적 소유권이 없음에도 심리적 소유권이 존재함을 강]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 심리적 소유권의 양면성을 설명합니다. 세미콜론 이후 '세입자들이 임대 공간을 개인화하면 만족도와 관리 습관이 개선되지만, 이사할 때 더 큰 고통을 경험한다'고 명시합니다. 마지막 문장은 '심리적 애착은 즉각적 웰빙과 책임을 향상시킬 수 있지만, 동시에 미래의 실망과 손실에 대한 취약성을 증가시킬 수 있다'고 정리합니다. 따라서 빈칸은 '긍정적 결과와 부정적 결과 모두를 만들어낼 수 있다'는 의미의 ③번이 정답입니다.
R-0154 56번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
The endowment effect describes a cognitive bias where people assign higher value to items they already possess than to identical items they do not own. This phenomenon has been extensively documented across numerous studies, from simple laboratory experiments with coffee mugs to real-world scenarios involving real estate. Research shows that once we acquire something, we tend to demand significantly more money to sell it than we would have paid to buy it initially. Some economists argue this reflects rational behavior rooted in loss aversion—the psychological principle that losses loom larger than equivalent gains. However, critics contend that ___________. They suggest the effect reveals irrational attachment rather than economic logic, as the item's intrinsic utility remains unchanged after purchase. Understanding whether the endowment effect represents rational decision-making or cognitive distortion has important implications for how we design markets and consumer policies.
✓ ①rational loss aversion cannot fully explain all instances of the endowment effect
②the endowment effect disappears when people have sufficient information about prices[정보 충분성에 대한 언급은 지문에서 제시되지 않으며, 비판의 핵심과 무관하다.]
③consumers are generally more loss-averse when purchasing luxury goods than necessities[사치재와 필수재의 구분은 endowment effect 논쟁의 중심주제가 아니다.]
④the endowment effect is more pronounced in cultures with stronger property rights protections[문화적 차이는 지문에서 다루지 않는 별개의 변수이다.]
⑤loss aversion is primarily a biological adaptation that enhances survival in uncertain environments[생물학적 적응은 loss aversion 자체를 설명하는 것이지, 비판자의 반박 주장이 아]
▶ 해설 보기
지문의 논리 흐름을 보면, '경제학자들은 손실회피로 설명한다'는 주장 다음에 'However(그러나) 비판자들은 주장한다'는 대조 표현이 나온다. 빈칸은 비판자들의 반박 주장이 들어가야 하므로, 손실회피로는 설명할 수 없는 측면이 있다는 의미의 ①번이 정답이다. 뒤의 문장 '효과는 비이성적 애착을 드러낸다'와도 논리적으로 연결된다.
R-0155 57번 · 빈칸 추론 ✔ 감수
Metacognition—thinking about one's own thinking—has emerged as a crucial factor in academic achievement and problem-solving success. Students who regularly reflect on their understanding, monitor their comprehension, and adjust their study strategies accordingly demonstrate significantly higher performance than those who study passively. Research in cognitive psychology reveals that effective learners constantly evaluate whether they truly understand material or merely recognize it. However, many students overestimate their mastery through a phenomenon called the illusion of competence. They mistake familiarity with actual comprehension. Notably, ___________. This explains why students sometimes fail exams despite feeling confident during preparation. Educators increasingly recognize that teaching metacognitive strategies—such as self-questioning, concept mapping, and spaced retrieval practice—is essential for developing independent, lifelong learners who can adapt to new challenges and transfer knowledge across different domains.
①passive study methods can be effective when combined with distributed practice schedules[분산 연습은 효과적이지만, 수동적 학습의 정당화는 illusion of competence]
✓ ②increased test anxiety often leads to improved metacognitive awareness and self-monitoring
③merely reviewing familiar material creates a false sense of understanding without genuine learning
④students with higher IQ scores naturally develop stronger metacognitive skills than their peers[IQ는 metacognition과의 직접적 인과관계가 명확하지 않으며, 지문의 학습 메커니]
⑤metacognitive abilities develop automatically as students progress through higher education levels[자동적 발달은 지문이 강조하는 '교육의 필요성'과 모순된다.]
▶ 해설 보기
지문은 '친숙함을 실제 이해와 착각한다'는 illusion of competence를 설명한 후 'Notably(특히)' 으로 이를 구체화하는 문장이 빈칸이다. 친숙한 자료를 검토하는 것이 실제 학습 없이 거짓 이해감을 만든다는 ③번이 앞 문장의 'mistake familiarity with actual comprehension'을 직접적으로 확장한다.

R-0006 58번 · 문장 삽입 ✔ 감수
삽입 문장: However, the allocation of limited urban land for such purposes remains a persistent challenge for city authorities.
The concept of urban green spaces has gained significant attention among city planners and environmental scientists. (①) Green areas such as parks and gardens provide multiple benefits to urban residents, including improved air quality and mental health advantages. Research indicates that exposure to natural environments reduces stress levels and enhances cognitive function in individuals working in high-pressure occupations. (②) The implementation of vertical gardens on building facades represents an innovative solution to space constraints in densely populated metropolitan areas. (③) These structures not only maximize ecological efficiency but also contribute to aesthetic urban design. Furthermore, urban forests play a crucial role in mitigating the heat island effect, which causes temperatures in cities to rise significantly compared to surrounding rural regions. (④) Studies have documented that trees and vegetation absorb solar radiation and facilitate evapotranspiration, thereby regulating microclimate conditions. (⑤) Consequently, municipalities worldwide are investing in comprehensive green infrastructure projects to ensure sustainable urban development and improve quality of life for their inhabitants.

⑤[⑤에 삽입하면 결론 부분에 새로운 문제점을 제시하여 해결책에 대한 논의를 방해합니다.]

R-0009 61번 · 문장 삽입 ✔ 감수
삽입 문장: Consequently, understanding the mechanisms underlying circadian regulation has become increasingly important for occupational health specialists and organizational policymakers.
The phenomenon of circadian rhythms has profound implications for human health and work performance across various occupational sectors. Biological clocks regulate sleep-wake cycles through the secretion of melatonin and cortisol, hormones that fluctuate according to light-dark cycles. (①) Modern industrial societies, however, frequently impose temporal demands that conflict with these natural physiological cycles. (②) Shift workers and frequent travelers experience significant disruption to their circadian homeostasis, resulting in compromised immune function and metabolic disorders. (③) Research has established strong correlations between circadian misalignment and increased vulnerability to cardiovascular diseases, obesity, and depression. (④) Organizations implementing chronobiological principles in workplace scheduling have documented measurable improvements in employee productivity and occupational safety outcomes. (⑤) These findings suggest that aligning human activity patterns with biological predispositions represents a pragmatic approach to organizational management and public health promotion.
①[①에 삽입하면 순환 리듬 기본 설명 직후 갑자기 결론을 제시하여 논리 전개가 부족합니다.]
②[②에 삽입하면 교대 근무 영향 설명 중간에 결론을 끼워 넣어 인과관계가 명확하지 않습니다.]

⑤[⑤에 삽입하면 최종 결론 부분에서 또 다른 결론을 제시하여 중복되고 약해집니다.]
▶ 해설 보기
'Consequently'는 앞의 부정적 건강 결과(심혈관질환, 비만, 우울증)에 기반한 결론입니다. ④번 위치(건강 위험 제시 후)에 삽입하면 이러한 문제점들이 중요한 이유를 설명하며 자연스럽게 해결책으로 전환됩니다.
R-0010 62번 · 문장 삽입 ✔ 감수
삽입 문장: This capacity extends beyond motor recovery, encompassing profound changes in cognitive architecture and behavioral repertoires.
The phenomenon of neuroplasticity has revolutionized contemporary understanding of brain function and cognitive development throughout the human lifespan. Traditional neuroscience maintained that neural structures remained relatively fixed after early childhood, severely limiting potential for recovery from brain injury. (①) However, contemporary research utilizing advanced neuroimaging technologies has conclusively demonstrated that the brain retains substantial capacity for reorganization and functional adaptation. (②) Stroke patients undergoing intensive rehabilitation therapy exhibit remarkable recovery of motor and linguistic functions through compensatory activation of alternative neural pathways. (③) Similarly, musicians and bilingual individuals demonstrate morphological brain changes resulting from sustained cognitive engagement and practice. (④) These adaptive mechanisms suggest that environmental stimulation and deliberate practice constitute primary determinants of neural development. (⑤) Therefore, educational institutions and therapeutic programs must incorporate evidence-based strategies that systematically leverage neuroplastic mechanisms to optimize human potential and facilitate recovery from neurological dysfunction.
①[①에 삽입하면 신경가소성의 기본 개념만 제시된 상태에서 확대된 설명을 끼워 넣어 시기상 부]
②[②에 삽입하면 운동 회복 사례 직후 갑자기 더 넓은 범위를 주장하여 논리 흐름이 자연스럽지]

⑤[⑤에 삽입하면 'Therefore'의 결론 앞에 새로운 주장을 끼워 넣어 인과관계가 약해집]
▶ 해설 보기
주어진 문장의 'This capacity'는 신경가소성의 재편성 능력을 지칭합니다. ④번 위치(음악가와 다언어 사용자의 뇌 변화 설명 후)에 삽입하면 구체적 사례에서 일반화된 결론으로 자연스럽게 전환되며, 신경 적응 메커니즘을 종합합니다.
R-0011 63번 · 문장 삽입 ✔ 감수
삽입 문장: However, this therapeutic aspect of nostalgia was largely overlooked during earlier periods when the emotion was primarily pathologized.
The concept of nostalgia has evolved significantly over centuries. ① Originally, nostalgia was classified as a medical condition affecting Swiss mercenaries who longed for their homeland. ② During the 19th century, scholars began to recognize it as a psychological phenomenon rather than a physical disease. ③ This shift in perspective fundamentally altered how society understood human emotions and memory. The term itself derives from Greek words meaning 'homecoming' and 'pain,' reflecting the bittersweet nature of reminiscing about the past. ④ Modern psychologists argue that nostalgia serves important functions in maintaining psychological well-being and social cohesion. Research demonstrates that nostalgic experiences can enhance mood, strengthen interpersonal relationships, and provide a sense of continuity in one's identity. ⑤ Museums and cultural institutions have increasingly recognized this phenomenon, designing exhibits that deliberately evoke nostalgic responses among visitors. Understanding nostalgia's role in human psychology has profound implications for how we approach mental health and cultural preservation in contemporary society.

Climate change represents one of the most pressing environmental challenges of our time. ① Scientists have accumulated extensive empirical data demonstrating rising global temperatures and their cascading effects on ecosystems worldwide. ② The burning of fossil fuels releases greenhouse gases that accumulate in the atmosphere, creating a thermal blanket effect. ③ This phenomenon has prompted governments and corporations to explore renewable energy alternatives and implement mitigation strategies. ④ Countries that have invested heavily in solar and wind technology have demonstrated significant reductions in carbon emissions. ⑤ However, the transition away from fossil fuels remains economically challenging for developing nations that depend on traditional energy sources for industrial growth. The disparity between developed and developing countries in addressing climate change raises complex questions about equity and global responsibility.
